Coordinate descent details:
Given h. For each i, compute coefficients a_l and b_l for all T_l as functions of h_i. Then solve 1D minimization. Since objective max over l. Number of lags m=2n-1. For each coordinate i, h_i appears in:
- T_0: h_i(1-h_i) -> -h_i^2 + h_i.
- For positive lag l = j - i >0: term includes h_i (1 - h_j) if j=i+l <n -> coefficient + (1-h_j), constant 0.
- For positive lag l = i - j? For positive lag l, term T_l = ∑ h_p (1-h_{p+l}). h_i can be first at p=i if i+l<n with coeff + (1-h_{i+l}); or second at p=i-l if i-l>=0 with coeff - h_{i-l}.
- For negative lag -l, equivalent T_{-l}=∑ h_{p-l}(1-h_p)? np.correlate full indexing: correlate(a,b)[k] = ∑ a_i b_{i+k-(n-1)}? Terms with a=h,b=1-h. For any lag d (positive means a before b?), h_i pairs with h_{i+d} as a_i(1-h_{i+d}) for d>0; and h_{i-d}(1-h_i) coefficient -h_{i-d}. Similarly. So for each offset d≠0 with |d|<n: if h_i is first (partner i±d in range): contribution (1 - h_partner) h_i. If second: contribution - h_partner h_i. Coeff c_i,d = (1 - h_partner) for right partner, -h_partner for left partner? For pair distance d:
If j=i+d in range: in lag +d term: h_i(1-h_j) coeff +(1-h_j).
If j=i-d in range: in lag -d term: h_j(1-h_i) coeff -h_j.
These are distinct lags, so separate lines. Thus at most 2 lines for each d where type? Actually both if both neighbors exist: two lines.

Plus T_0 quadratic.
Objective φ(y)=max( y - y^2 + const0, max_{lines} (a_r y + b_r) ), const0 is sum of other products in T0: ∑_{m≠i} h_m(1-h_m). Lines: for right neighbor j=i+d: T_{d} other = const + (1-h_j)y; left neighbor j=i-d: T_{-d} other = const - h_j y. Constants: for each such lag, sum over all p except p=i (or except p=i-d?) We can compute current T_l values and subtract contributions involving h_i to get constants.

For each coordinate i, current contributions:
- T0 current includes h_i(1-h_i). const0 = T0_cur - h_i(1-h_i).
- For right lag d (j=i+d): T_d includes h_i(1-h_j). const_d = T_d_cur - h_i(1-h_j). line coeff a=1-h_j, b=const_d.
- For left lag -d? If j=i-d, term T_{-d} includes h_j(1-h_i) current = h_j(1-h_i). const = T_{-d} - h_j(1-h_i). line coeff a=-h_j, b=const.

Could we update T incrementally for O(n) per coordinate? Maybe possible: changing h_i affects T_l for l where pair involving i: T0 and two sets of lags? For each right neighbor j, T_{j-i} changes by (1-h_j)*(y - h_i). For each left neighbor j, T_{j-i} (negative lag) changes by -h_j*(y - h_i). T0 changes by y-y^2 - (h_i-h_i^2). This makes coordinate descent fast and exact.

Implement pair_update(i,j):
Inputs h,T,C.
i<j for simplicity (choose i, j=i+1 maybe). Let a_old=h[i], b_old=h[j], s=a_old+b_old. Need domain a∈[lower,upper] where 0≤a≤1, 0≤b=s-a≤1 => a∈[max(0,s-1), min(1,s)]. If interval empty? s∈[0,2]. Always.
We need construct constraints as quadratic/linear in a.
Constants based on current T and current old contribution.
For all terms affected by i or j:
- T0: original T0 includes a(1-a)+b(1-b). New Q0 = T0 - a(1-a)-b(1-b) + a(1-a)+b(1-b) with b=s-a.
So Q0(a) = c0 + a - a^2 + (s-a) - (s-a)^2 = c0 + s - [a^2+(s-a)^2]. Expand: a^2+(s-a)^2=2a^2-2sa+s^2. Q0 = c0 + s - s^2 -2a^2 +2s a. So p0=-2, q0=2s, r0=c0 + s - s^2. Since a+b=s exact.

Feasibility for v over a∈[A,B] (bounds from mass/bounds):

Single coordinate exact subproblem (without mass) is easy and fast:
Constraints:
- T0: q(a)=a-a^2 + c0, c0=T0 - old a(1-a).
- For every neighbor u (all v≠i), there are two directed terms:
   If v>i: lag +d. Term1: h_i(1-h_v) slope (1-h_v). Term2: T_{-d}: h_v(1-h_i) slope -h_v.
   If v<i: lag -d. Term1: h_i(1-h_v) slope (1-h_v) (first with left). Term2: T_{+d}: h_v(1-h_i) slope -h_v.
For each, constant = T_lag - old directed contribution. Positive slopes from first terms, negative slopes from second terms.

Pair update exact might be worth implementing. Let's derive general pair formula in a systematic way using a small helper that for a given lag index and old variables constructs p,q,r for new a.

We have two variable indices i,j (i<j), old values x=h[i], y=h[j], s=x+y, new a, b=s-a. For each term T_l, it is sum over pairs (u, v=u+l) within bounds? We can express T_l as sum of directed edge contributions. An edge is ordered pair (u,v) such that v = u + l? For l can negative. But np.correlate full indexing: For lag l (integer, -n+1..n-1), term = dx ∑_{u: 0≤u<n, 0≤u+l<n} h_u (1 - h_{u+l}). Thus ordered edges from u to u+l. If l=0, edge u->u.
For a given lag index m, l=m-(n-1). The term is sum over u in max(0,-l) to min(n-1,n-1-l) of h_u*(1-h_{u+l}).
For pair update, this sum may have 0,1,or2 variable occurrences? Since each edge is an ordered pair. It can include:
- self edge if l=0 and u=i or j: T0 contributions for i and j.
- edge involving both if {i,j} and l = j-i (edge i->j) or l = i-j (edge j->i).
- edge involving i with fixed k (k not j): u=i or u+l=i.
- edge involving j similarly.
No edge can involve both variables more than one? For pair, the two directed edges between i,j appear in two different lags (l=j-i and l=i-j).
Thus for each lag, contribution as function of a,b can be at most:
- If lag 0: c0 + f_i(a)+f_j(b), f(z)=z(1-z), concave sum -> quadratic p=-2.
- If lag = j-i: includes edge i->j: a(1-b) plus fixed edges possibly involving i with other? Wait for a given lag l=j-i, the sum includes edges u->u+l. It could include edge i->j, and also if i+l? For other u involving i? For u = i? yes i->j. For u = i-l? if i-l in range, edge (i-l)->i involves i as second. For j as first? j -> j+l (if in range) involves j. For j as second? j-l = i, that's the pair. Other? Because lag fixed; each variable appears in at most one edge in the sum? For lag l>0, variable p can be first at u=p (if p+l<n) and second at u=p-l (if p-l>=0). These are two edges in same lag: one outgoing, one incoming. If both p and q=i/j, could there be more than two edges? If n large and lag d, i and j distance d. In lag d:
   - edge i->j (pair)

Let's implement T update for single variable p old->new with all four directed contributions.
Given current h (before changing p) and T current. For d=1..n-1:
  coeff_plus for T_{+d} = 0
  if p+d<n: coeff_plus += 1 - h[p+d]
  if p-d>=0: coeff_plus -= h[p-d]
  T[n-1+d] += coeff_plus*(new-old)
  coeff_minus for T_{-d} = 0
  if p-d>=0: coeff_minus += 1 - h[p-d]
  if p+d<n: coeff_minus -= h[p+d]
  T[n-1-d] += coeff_minus*(new-old)
T0 += new-new^2 - old-old^2.
Then set h[p]=new.
Check for pair sequential:
 old values x,y. Set h[i]=a (update T with old x). Then set h[j]=b (update T with old y, but h[i] already a). Does this correctly update pair edge a(1-b)? Let's test.
Initial T includes x(1-y) in T_d and y(1-x) in T_-d.
Update i x->a:
 T_d coeff_plus for d? p=i, d=j-i: p+d=j -> + (1-y); p-d maybe. So T_d += (1-y)(a-x) => now a(1-y) minus original? Original + (1-y)(a-x) = x(1-y)+(1-y)(a-x)=a(1-y). Good.
 T_-d coeff_minus for d: p=i, p-d? not j; p+d=j -> coeff_minus -= h[j]= -y. T_-d += -y(a-x) => original y(1-x) + -y(a-x)= y(1-a). Good (pair reverse updated with new a).

Maybe easier: Since only two variables change, the change in T_l relative to old T is sum over affected edges of delta. We can compute the new T_l as old T_l + delta_l(a). Delta_l is linear/quadratic in a. We need constraints f_l(a)=T_l + delta_l(a) ≤v. Delta_l can be expressed as P_l a^2 + Q_l a + D_l, where D_l chosen so f(old a)=T_l. Instead of full constant, we can compute P_l, Q_l, D_l such that delta_l(a) = P_l(a^2 - x^2) + Q_l(a - x)? This automatically f(old)=T_l. Then constraint P_l a^2+Q_l a + (T_l - P_l x^2 - Q_l x) ≤v. R = T_l - (P_l x^2+Q_l x). This avoids computing old_value and C separately. Good. Need P_l and Q_l for delta relative to old a,b=s-a.
For constraint, if P_l=0, Q_l linear delta slope. If P_l=1 for pair lag? For a(1-b): expression a(1-s+a) = a^2 +(1-s)a. Relative to old x: delta = (a^2-x^2)+(1-s)(a-x). Thus P=1,Q=1-s. For b(1-a): a^2 -(s+1)a +s. Relative: (a^2-x^2) -(s+1)(a-x) because constant s cancels? Check delta = [a^2 -(s+1)a +s] - [x^2 -(s+1)x +s] = (a^2-x^2) -(s+1)(a-x). P=1,Q=-(s+1).
For linear term a(1-hv): delta=(1-hv)(a-x), P=0,Q=1-hv.
For h_u(1-a): delta= -h_u(a-x), Q=-h_u.
For b(1-hv): delta= -(1-hv)(a-x), Q=-(1-hv).
For h_u(1-b): delta= h_u(a-x), Q=h_u.
Thus for each lag, P is 1 if pair directed edge present (one per special lag), else 0. Q is sum of coefficients for all edges involving i/j (including pair directional coefficient) relative to a. R = T_l - (P*x^2 + Q*x) (since delta=P(a^2-x^2)+Q(a-x); f=T_l + delta = P a^2+Q a + T_l - P x^2 - Q x).

Let's derive formulas for Q_l for pair. Let i<j.
For positive lag l=d>0:
Edges:
- i first to i+d: if i+d<n. If i+d==j: P=1, Q += 1-s. else Q += 1 - h[i+d]
- j first to j+d: if j+d<n. If j+d==i impossible. If j+d maybe? fixed: Q += -(1 - h[j+d]) = h[j+d]-1
- i second from i-d: if i-d>=0. (edge i-d -> i): Q += -h[i-d]
- j second from j-d: if j-d>=0. If j-d==i: P=1,Q += 1-s (same as first pair; for d=1) duplicate; if d!=1 fixed: Q += -h[j-d]
For d=j-i, pair edge present once: P=1, Q += 1-s. The two candidate conditions i+d==j and j-d==i both true; only add once. Also i first and j second are same. For d != di, no pair.
For negative lag l=-d:
- i first to i-d: if i-d>=0: Q += 1 - h[i-d] (since i first left) (if i-d==j impossible)
- j first to j-d: if j-d>=0. If j-d==i: P=1,Q += -(s+1); else fixed: Q += -(1 - h[j-d]) = h[j-d]-1
- i second from i+d: if i+d<n. If i+d==j: P=1,Q += -(s+1) duplicate; else fixed: Q += -h[i+d]
- j second from j+d: if j+d<n: Q += h[j+d] (since j second, wrt a +h_u)
For d=di, pair edge once P=1,Q += -(s+1).

For Qpos (l=+d):
  # i first right: valid d <= n-1-i. If d == di -> pair; else 1-h[i+d]

Let's define `project_feasible` robust:
def project_feasible(h,n):
  target=n/2.0
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # iterative sum adjust as previous
  for _ in range(200):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
       room=1-h; s=room.sum()
       if s<1e-14: break
       h = h + diff/s*room
    else:
       s=h.sum()
       if s<1e-14: break
       h = h + diff/s*h
  return np.clip(h,0,1)
But if diff negative, subtract proportionally from h. At end sum close target; can force exact by adjusting a small coordinate? The evaluation constraints maybe sum exactly? Let's implement build constraints carefully with indexing.
T length m=2n-1, center c=n-1.
Tpos = T[n:] length D? T indices n..2n-2 inclusive length n-1. Yes.
Tneg = T[:n-1] length D indices 0..n-2 correspond negative lags -1..-(n-1)? Wait Tneg slice T[:c] = indices 0..n-2. Index for lag -d is c-d = n-1-d. For d=1 index n-2, which is last of Tneg. Our Tneg array order if we slice T[:n-1] is index 0 (lag -(n-1)) to n-2 (lag -1). But Qneg array indexed d=1..n-1. Need align! Important.
For positive: T indices c+d: d=1 -> n, ..., d=n-1 -> 2n-2. Tpos slice T[n:2*n-1] order d=1..n-1. Good.
For negative: T index c-d = n-1-d. For d=1 -> n-2; d=n-1 ->0. Our Qneg[d-1] corresponds d. So Tneg aligned with Qneg should be T[n-2::-1] (d=1 last to 0), not T[:n-1]. Similarly when setting incident mask, need assign Tneg array reversed.
So:
Tpos = T[c+1:]  # length D, d increasing.

`pair_check` parameters:
```
def pair_check(v, lo, hi, Qp, Rp, Qn, Rn, Rz, Pcv, Qcv, Rcv, Pcn, Qcn, Rcn, tol=1e-11):
    if Rz.size and float(Rz.max()) > v + tol: return False
    if Qp.size:
        up = float(np.min((v - Rp)/Qp))
        if up < hi: hi = up
    if Qn.size:
        low = float(np.max((v - Rn)/Qn))  # Qn negative
        if low > lo: lo = low
    if Pcv.size:
       disc = Qcv*Qcv - 4.0*Pcv*(Rcv - v)
       if float(np.min(disc)) < -1e-10: return False
       disc = np.maximum(disc,0.0)
       rt1 = (-Qcv - np.sqrt(disc))/(2.0*Pcv)
       rt2 = (-Qcv + np.sqrt(disc))/(2.0*Pcv)
       left = np.minimum(rt1, rt2); right=np.maximum(rt1,rt2)
       lom = float(np.max(left))
       him = float(np.min(right))
       if lom > lo: lo = lom
       if him < hi: hi = him
    if hi < lo - tol: return False
    if Pcn.size:
       # process each concave; only one but loop
       for idx in range(Pcn.size):
           A = -Pcn[idx]; Q = Qcn[idx]; R = Rcn[idx]
           disc = Q*Q - 4.0*A*(v - R)
           if disc > 1e-11:
              sd = math.sqrt(disc)
              r1 = (Q - sd)/(2.0*A)
              r2 = (Q + sd)/(2.0*A)
              if not (lo <= r1 + tol or hi >= r2 - tol):
                  return False
       # else all
    return True
```
Use math.sqrt scalar for concave. For vector convex use np.
Potential issue: For concave, if disc>0 but roots from quadratic A a^2 -Q a +(v-R). If the inequality is ≥0 outside. Yes.
Test T0 P=-2,Q=2,R=T0-0.5 for old x=0.5. Suppose v=0 (minimum? T0 others?). f=-2a^2+2a+R. For v=0.0? disc=4 -8*(v-R). If R=-0.5? disc=4-8*0.5=0 all? If f≤0 all? f= -2a^2+2a-0.5 = -(a-0.5)^2? ≤0 all yes. Good.

Function signatures:
```
def attempt_pair(i,j,h,T,n,C,Qpos,Qneg,Ppos,Pneg,Parr,Qarr,Rarr):
    D=n-1
    if i==j: return False,C
    if i>j: i,j=j,i
    x=h[i]; y=h[j]; s=x+y
    al=... ah=...
    if ah-al < 1e-14: return False,C
    # zero buffers
    Qpos[:]=0.0; Qneg[:]=0.0; Ppos[:]=0.0; Pneg[:]=0.0
    ... fill
    di=...; dii=...
    # build constraint arrays
    P0=-2.0; Q0=2.0*s; R0=T[n-1] - (P0*x*x + Q0*x)
    Parr[0]=P0; Qarr[0]=Q0; Rarr[0]=R0
    k=1
    maskp = (Ppos != 0.0) | (np.abs(Qpos) > 1e-15)
    cnp=int(maskp.sum())
    if cnp:
       Parr[k:k+cnp]=Ppos[maskp]
       Qarr[k:k+cnp]=Qpos[maskp]
       Tpos=T[n:2*n-1]
       Rarr[k:k+cnp] = Tpos[maskp] - (Ppos[maskp]*x*x + Qpos[maskp]*x)
       k+=cnp
    maskn = (Pneg != 0.0) | (np.abs(Qneg) > 1e-15)
    cnn=int(maskn.sum())
    if cnn:
       Parr[k:k+cnn]=Pneg[maskn]
       Qarr[k:k+cnn]=Qneg[maskn]
       Tneg=T[:n-1][::-1]
       Rarr[k:k+cnn] = Tneg[maskn] - (Pneg[maskn]*x*x + Qneg[maskn]*x)
       k+=cnn
    # unaff mask
    inc = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=bool)
    inc[n-1]=True
    if cnp:
       idxp = np.arange(n,2*n-1)[maskp]
       inc[idxp]=True
    if cnn:
       idxn = c - np.arange(1,n)[maskn]
       inc[idxn]=True
    C_unaff = float(T[~inc].max()) if (~inc).any() else 0.0
    Parr[k]=0.0; Qarr[k]=0.0; Rarr[k]=C_unaff; k+=1
    low = C_unaff - 1e-10
    if low < -1e-9: low = -1e-9
    high = C
    if high - low <= 1e-12: return False,C
    # subarrays for check
    P=Parr[:k]; Q=Qarr[:k]; R=Rarr[:k]
    conv = P > 1e-12
    conc = P < -1e-12
    lin = ~(conv | conc)
    linpos = lin & (Q > 1e-14)
    linneg = lin & (Q < -1e-14)
    linzero = lin & ~(linpos | linneg)
    Qp=Q[linpos]; Rp=R[linpos]
    Qn=Q[linneg]; Rn=R[linneg]
    Rz=R[linzero]
    Pcv=P[conv]; Qcv=Q[conv]; Rcv=R[conv]
    Pcn=P[conc]; Qcn=Q[conc]; Rcn=R[conc]
    # bisection
    for _ in range(34):
       mid=0.5*(low+high)
       if pair_check(mid,al,ah,Qp,Rp,Qn,Rn,Rz,Pcv,Qcv,Rcv,Pcn,Qcn,Rcn):
          high=mid
       else:
          low=mid
       if high-low < 1e-13: break
    a=pair_choose(high, al,ah,x,Qp,Rp,Qn,Rn,Rz,Pcv,Qcv,Rcv,Pcn,Qcn,Rcn)
    if a is None: return False,C
    if a < al: a=al
    elif a>ah: a=ah
    b=s-a
    if b<0 or b>1:
       # adjust
       if b<0: b=0; a=s
       else: b=1; a=s-1
    if abs(a-x) < 1e-13:
       return False,C
    y_old=h[j]
    update_T_single(i,a,h,T,n)
    update_T_single(j,b,h,T,n)
    Cnew=float(T.max())
    if Cnew < C - 1e-13:
       return True, Cnew
    # revert
    update_T_single(j,y_old,h,T,n)
    update_T_single(i,x,h,T,n)
    return False,C
```

Time management details:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try: h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h)
    except: n=256; h=np.full(n,0.5)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    T=compute_T(h,n)
    C=float(T.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=C; best_n=n
    # allocate buffers for n
    cap=2*n+5
    Parr=np.empty(cap); Qarr=np.empty(cap); Rarr=np.empty(cap)
    Qpos=np.empty(n-1); Qneg=np.empty(n-1); Ppos=np.empty(n-1); Pneg=np.empty(n-1)
    # main adjacent sweeps time to 400s
    sweep_deadline=t0+350
    improved_sweeps=0
    while time.time() < sweep_deadline:
       order=rng.permutation(n-1)
       imp=0
       for t in order:
          if time.time() > sweep_deadline: break
          ok,Cn=attempt_pair(t,t+1,h,T,n,C,Qpos,Qneg,Ppos,Pneg,Parr,Qarr,Rarr)
          if ok:
             C=Cn; imp+=1
             if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
       if imp==0:
          # try random permutation sweeps few
          break
    # random sweeps to 600s
    rand_deadline=t0+650
    no_imp = 0
    while time.time() < rand_deadline:
       perm=rng.permutation(n)
       imp=0
       for idx in range(n-1):
          i=int(perm[idx]); j=int(perm[idx+1])
          if time.time() > rand_deadline: break
          if abs(i-j)==1? okay; if i==j continue
          ok,Cn=attempt_pair(i,j,...)
          if ok: C=Cn; imp++; update best
       if imp==0:
          no_imp +=1
          if no_imp>=4: break
       else: no_imp=0
    # noise restarts to deadline-60
    restart_deadline=deadline-60
    levels=[0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.04]
    for level in levels:
       if time.time() > restart_deadline: break
       hp = best_h + level*rng.randn(n)
       hp=project_feasible(hp,n)
       Tp=compute_T(hp,n)
       Cp=float(Tp.max())
       # do adjacent sweeps for max 60s or until no imp

Now, update_T_single using h current. Let's implement vectorized? Loop is simple. But in Python, for d in range(1,n): computing if conditions each. For many updates, okay. Could vectorize to speed:
```
def update_T_single(p,newval,h,T,n):
    old=h[p]; delta=newval-old
    if delta==0: h[p]=newval; return
    c=n-1
    T[c] += (newval - newval*newval) - (old - old*old)
    # d arrays? We'll allocate d=np.arange(1,n) each call? Too much. Use loop.
    for d in range(1,n):
       cp=0.0; cm=0.0
       if d <= n-1-p:
          hr=h[p+d]
          cp += 1.0 - hr
          cm -= hr
       if d <= p:
          hl=h[p-d]
          cp -= hl
          cm += 1.0 - hl
       T[c+d] += cp*delta
       T[c-d] += cm*delta
    h[p]=newval
```
For n=400, loop 399. If 10k updates, 4M iterations, okay. Could precompute maxd? Fine.
But when updating pair sequentially, for j after i, h[p+d] maybe out; okay.
Need T indices exist c-d for d=c? if d=n-1, c-d=0; c-d ≥0. c+d=2n-2. good.

Compute T function:
```
def make_T(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx
```
`np.correlate` returns length 2n-1. Order? It matches. Good.

Project feasible: Make sure if n maybe odd, target .5. Use iterative. At end, to enforce exact, maybe:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; sroom=room.sum()
          if sroom<1e-15: break
          h=h+diff/sroom*room
       else:
          sh=h.sum()
          if sh<1e-15: break
          h=h+diff/sh*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=np.nonzero(h<1)[0]
       if idx.size: h[idx[0]] += diff
    else:
       idx=np.nonzero(h>0)[0]
       if idx.size: h[idx[0]] += diff
    return h
```
But adding diff to one coordinate may break [0,1] if diff larger; after iterative diff tiny. But if initial far, diff maybe; use robust iterative. Good.

Now, implement run time sketches:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-4
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h)
    except Exception:
       n=256; h=np.full(n,0.5)
    if n < 4: n=256; h=np.full(n,0.5)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    T=make_T(h,n); C=float(T.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=C; best_n=n
    cap=2*n+10
    Pbuf=np.empty(cap); Qbuf=np.empty(cap); Rbuf=np.empty(cap)
    Qpos=np.empty(n-1); Qneg=np.empty(n-1); Ppos=np.empty(n-1); Pneg=np.empty(n-1)
    # function to run sweeps maybe inline.
    end_adj=t0+min(300,budget_s*0.3)
    stall=0
    while time.time() < end_adj:
       order=rng.permutation(n-1)
       imp=0
       for t in order:
          if time.time() > end_adj: break
          ok,Cn=attempt_pair(t,t+1,h,T,n,C,Qpos,Qneg,Ppos,Pneg,Pbuf,Qbuf,Rbuf)
          if ok:
             C=Cn; imp+=1
             if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
       if imp==0: stall+=1
       else: stall=0
       # recompute T to reduce drift
       T=make_T(h,n); C=float(T.max())
       if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy()
       if stall>=6: break
    end_rand=deadline - 120
    stall=0
    while time.time() < end_rand:
       perm=rng.permutation(n)
       imp=0
       for idx in range(n-1):
          if time.time() > end_rand: break
          i=int(perm[idx]); j=int(perm[idx+1])
          if i==j: continue
          ok,Cn=attempt_pair(i,j,h,T,n,C,...)
          if ok: ...
       T=make_T(h,n); C=float(T.max())
       update best
       if imp==0: stall+=1
       else: stall=0
       if stall>=8: break
    # restarts
    rest_end=deadline-40
    levels=[0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02]
    for lvl in levels:
       if time.time() > rest_end: break
       phase_end=min(rest_end, time.time()+80)
       hp=best_h + lvl*rng.randn(n)
       hp=project_feasible(hp,n)
       Tp=make_T(hp,n); Cp=float(Tp.max())
       # if Cp not much > best? attempt
       for _ in range(4):
          if time.time()>phase_end: break
          order=rng.permutation(n-1)
          imp=0
          for t in order:
             if time.time()>phase_end: break
             ok,Cn=attempt_pair(t,t+1,hp,Tp,n,Cp,... buffers)
             if ok:
                Cp=Cn; imp+=1
         Tp=make_T(hp,n); Cp=float(Tp.max())
         if imp==0: break
       if Cp<best_c:
          best_c=Cp; best_h=hp.copy(); best_n=n
          h=best_h.copy(); T=make_T(h,n); C=float(T.max())
    # final adjacent polish
    final_adj=deadline-6
    for _ in range(2):
       if time.time()>final_adj: break
       order=rng.permutation(n-1)
       for t in order:
          if time.time()>final_adj: break
          ok,Cn=attempt_pair(t,t+1,h,T,n,C,...)
          if ok:
             C=Cn
             if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
    T=make_T(h,n); C=float(T.max())
    if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy()
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)  # maybe best_n=n
    # if best_n != n? currently same. If we add multi-res maybe.
    Cbest=make_T(best_h,best_n).max()
    return best_h, float(Cbest), best_n
```

def fix_mass(h,n):
   target=n/2
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-10:
      # add to elements with room
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
      # proportional adjustment
      if diff>0:
         room=1-h; s=room.sum()
         h=h+diff/s*room if s>0 else h+diff/n
      else: ...

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def make_T(h,n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * (2.0/n)

def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; s=room.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h=h+diff/s*room
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h=h+diff/s*h
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
        idx=np.nonzero(h<1.0)[0]
        if idx.size: h[h? idx[0]] += diff
    elif diff<0:
        idx=np.nonzero(h>0.0)[0]
        if idx.size: h[idx[0]] += diff
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
Adding diff to one may push out of bounds if diff large; but iterative diff small. Good.

Implement:
```
def pair_bounds(v, al, ah, Qp, Rp, Qn, Rn, Rz, Pcv, Qcv, Rcv):
  lo=al; hi=ah; tol=1e-10
  if Rz.size and Rz.max() > v + tol: return None
  if Qp.size:
     hi=min(hi, float(np.min((v-Rp)/Qp)))
  if Qn.size:
     lo=max(lo, float(np.max((v-Rn)/Qn)))
  if Pcv.size:
     D=Qcv*Qcv - 4*Pcv*(Rcv-v)
     if D.min() < -1e-9: return None
     D=np.maximum(D,0); s=np.sqrt(D)
     r1=(-Qcv-s)/(2*Pcv); r2=(-Qcv+s)/(2*Pcv)
     lo=max(lo, float(np.max(np.minimum(r1,r2))))
     hi=min(hi, float(np.min(np.maximum(r1,r2))))
  if hi < lo - tol: return None
  return lo,hi
```
Then `pair_check`:
```
def pair_check(v, al, ah, Qp, Rp, Qn, Rn, Rz, Pcv, Qcv, Rcv, Pcn, Qcn, Rcn):
  b=pair_bounds(...)
  if b is None: return False
  lo,hi=b; tol=1e-10
  for idx in range(Pcn.size):
    A=-Pcn[idx]; Q=Qcn[idx]; R=Rcn[idx]
    disc=Q*Q - 4*A*(v-R)
    if disc > 1e-11:
      sd=math.sqrt(disc); r1=(Q-sd)/(2*A); r2=(Q+sd)/(2*A)
      if not (lo <= r1 + tol or hi >= r2 - tol): return False
  return True
```
`pair_choose`:
```
def pair_choose(v, al, ah, old, Qp, Rp, Qn, Rn, Rz, Pcv,Qcv,Rcv,Pcn,Qcn,Rcn):
  b=pair_bounds(...)
  if b is None: return None
  lo,hi=b; tol=1e-10
  intervals=[(lo,hi)]
  if Pcn.size:
    new=[]
    for t in range(Pcn.size):
      temp=[]
      A=-Pcn[t]; Q=Qcn[t]; R=Rcn[t]
      disc=Q*Q -4*A*(v-R)
      for L,U in intervals:
         if disc <= 1e-11:
            temp.append((L,U)); continue
         sd=math.sqrt(disc); r1=(Q-sd)/(2*A); r2=(Q+sd)/(2*A)
         if L <= r1 + tol and U >= L: temp.append((L, min(U,r1)))
         if U >= r2 - tol and U >= L: temp.append((max(L,r2), U))
      intervals=temp
      if not intervals: return None
  best=None; bd=1e100
  for L,U in intervals:
    L=max(L,al); U=min(U,ah)
    if U<L: continue
    if L <= old <= U: return old
    cand=L if old<L else U
    d=abs(cand-old)
    if d<bd: bd=d; best=cand
  if best is None: best=old if al<=old<=ah else 0.5*(al+ah)
  return best
```

```
def attempt_pair(i,j,h,T,n,C,Qp,Qn,Pp,Pn,Pb,Qb,Rb):
    if i==j: return False,C
    if i>j: i,j=j,i
    x=h[i]; y=h[j]; s=x+y
    al = s-1.0 if s>1.0 else 0.0
    ah = s if s<1.0 else 1.0
    if ah-al < 1e-13: return False,C
    D=n-1; c=n-1; di=j-i; q=di-1
    Qp[:]=0.0; Qn[:]=0.0; Pp[:]=0.0; Pn[:]=0.0
    L1=n-1-i; L2=n-1-j; L3=i; L4=j
    if L1>0: Qp[:L1] += 1.0 - h[i+1:n]
    Qp[q] += (1.0-s) - (1.0-h[j]); Pp[q]=1.0
    if L2>0: Qp[:L2] += h[j+1:n] - 1.0
    if L3>0: Qp[:L3] -= h[i-1::-1]
    if L4>0:
        Qp[:L4] += h[j-1::-1]
        Qp[q] -= h[i]
    if L3>0: Qn[:L3] += 1.0 - h[i-1::-1]
    if L4>0:
        Qn[:L4] += h[j-1::-1] - 1.0
        Qn[q] += (-(s+1.0)) - (h[i] - 1.0)
        Pn[q]=1.0
    if L1>0:
        Qn[:L1] -= h[i+1:n]
        Qn[q] += h[j]
    if L2>0: Qn[:L2] += h[j+1:n]
    Rb[0]=T[c] - (-2.0*x*x + 2.0*s*x)
    Qb[0]=2.0*s; Pb[0]=-2.0
    k=1
    Tpos=T[n:c+n] ??? T[n:2*n-1]
```

        mid=0.5*(low+hi)
        if pair_check(mid, al, ah, Q[lp],R[lp],Q[lng],R[lng],R[lz],P[conv],Q[conv],R[conv],P[conc],Q[conc],R[conc]): hi=mid
        else: low=mid
        if hi-low<1e-12: break
    a=pair_choose(min(C, hi+1e-11), al, ah, x, ...)
```

def update_T_single(p,newval,h,T,n):
 old=h[p]; delta=newval-old; h[p]=newval; c=n-1
 T[c]+=...
 for d in range(1,n):
   right = d <= n-1-p; left = d <= p
   small=0.0; large=0.0
   if right: small += 1.0 - h[p+d]; large -= h[p+d]
   if left: small -= h[p-d]; large += 1.0 - h[p-d]
   if _CORR_LARGE:

      T[c-d] += small*delta
   else:
      T[c+d] += small*delta
      T[c-d] += large*delta

```python
import numpy as np, time, math

def detect_orientation():
    a = np.array([0.0, 1.0, 1.0])
    b = np.array([1.0, 0.0, 0.0])
    c = np.correlate(a, b, mode="full")
    return float(c[-1]) > float(c[1])

_LARGE_CORR = detect_orientation()

def make_T(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)

def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + diff / s * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + diff / s * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
        idx = np.nonzero(h < 1.0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx[0]] += diff
    elif diff < 0:
        idx = np.nonzero(h > 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx[0]] += diff
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

def update_T_one(p, nv, h, T, n):
    ov = h[p]
    d = nv - ov
    if abs(d) < 1e-16:
        return
    c = n - 1
    T[c] += (nv - nv * nv) - (ov - ov * ov)
    rp = n - 1 - p
    lp = p
    for k in range(1, n):
        small = 0.0
        large = 0.0
        if k <= rp:
            hr = h[p + k]
            small += 1.0 - hr
            large -= hr
        if k <= lp:
            hl = h[p - k]
            small -= hl
            large += 1.0 - hl
        if _LARGE_CORR:
            T[c + k] += large * d
            T[c - k] += small * d
        else:
            T[c + k] += small * d
            T[c - k] += large * d
    h[p] = nv

def pair_bounds(v, al, ah, qp, rp, qn, rn, rz, cp, cq, cr):
    lo, hi = al, ah
    tol = 1e-10
    if rz.size and float(rz.max()) > v + tol:
        return None
    if qp.size:
        hi = min(hi, float(np.min((v - rp) / qp)))
    if qn.size:
        lo = max(lo, float(np.max((v - rn) / qn)))
    if cp.size:
        disc = cq * cq - 4.0 * cp * (cr - v)
        if float(disc.min()) < -1e-9:
            return None
        disc = np.maximum(disc, 0.0)
        sd = np.sqrt(disc)
        r1 = (-cq - sd) / (2.0 * cp)
        r2 = (-cq + sd) / (2.0 * cp)
        lo = max(lo, float(np.max(np.minimum(r1, r2))))
        hi = min(hi, float(np.min(np.maximum(r1, r2))))
    if hi < lo - tol:
        return None
    return lo, hi

def pair_check(v, al, ah, qp, rp, qn, rn, rz, cp, cq, cr, cn, qc, rc):
    b = pair_bounds(v, al, ah, qp, rp, qn, rn, rz, cp, cq, cr)
    if b is None:
        return False
    lo, hi = b
    tol = 1e-10
    for t in range(cn.size):
        A = -cn[t]
        Q = qc[t]
        R = rc[t]
        disc = Q * Q - 4.0 * A * (v - R)
        if disc > 1e-11:
            sd = math.sqrt(disc)
            r1 = (Q - sd) / (2.0 * A)
            r2 = (Q + sd) / (2.0 * A)
            if not (lo <= r1 + tol or hi >= r2 - tol):
                return False
    return True

def pair_choose(v, al, ah, old, qp, rp, qn, rn, rz, cp, cq, cr, cn, qc, rc):
    b = pair_bounds(v, al, ah, qp, rp, qn, rn, rz, cp, cq, cr)
    if b is None:
        return None
    lo, hi = b
    tol = 1e-10
    intervals = [(lo, hi)]
    if cn.size:
        for t in range(cn.size):
            A = -cn[t]
            Q = qc[t]
            R = rc[t]
            disc = Q * Q - 4.0 * A * (v - R)
            nxt = []
            for L, U in intervals:
                if L > U:
                    continue
                if disc <= 1e-11:
                    nxt.append((L, U))
                else:
                    sd = math.sqrt(disc)
                    r1 = (Q - sd) / (2.0 * A)
                    r2 = (Q + sd) / (2.0 * A)
                    if L <= r1 + tol:
                        nxt.append((L, min(U, r1)))
                    if U >= r2 - tol:
                        nxt.append((max(L, r2), U))
            intervals = nxt
            if not intervals:
                return None
    best = None
    bd = 1e100
    for L, U in intervals:
        L = max(L, al)
        U = min(U, ah)
        if U < L:
            continue
        if L <= old <= U:
            return old
        cand = L if old < L else U
        dd = abs(cand - old)
        if dd < bd:
            bd = dd
            best = cand
    if best is None:
        best = old if al <= old <= ah else 0.5 * (al + ah)
    return best

def attempt_pair(i, j, h, T, n, C, Qs, Qn, Ps, Pn, Pbuf, Qbuf, Rbuf):
    if i == j:
        return False, C
    if i > j:
        i, j = j, i
    x = h[i]
    y = h[j]
    s = x + y
    al = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    ah = min(1.0, s)
    if ah - al < 1e-13:
        return False, C
    D = n - 1
    c = n - 1
    di = j - i
    q = di - 1
    Qs[:D] = 0.0
    Qn[:D] = 0.0
    Ps[:D] = 0.0
    Pn[:D] = 0.0
    L1 = n - 1 - i
    L2 = n - 1 - j
    L3 = i
    L4 = j
    if L1 > 0:
        Qs[:L1] += 1.0 - h[i + 1:n]
    Qs[q] += (1.0 - s) - (1.0 - h[j])
    Ps[q] = 1.0
    if L2 > 0:
        Qs[:L2] += h[j + 1:n] - 1.0
    if L3 > 0:
        Qs[:L3] -= h[i - 1::-1]
    if L4 > 0:
        Qs[:L4] += h[j - 1::-1]
        Qs[q] -= h[i]
    if L3 > 0:
        Qn[:L3] += 1.0 - h[i - 1::-1]
    if L4 > 0:
        Qn[:L4] += h[j - 1::-1] - 1.0
        Qn[q] += (-(s + 1.0)) - (h[i] - 1.0)
        Pn[q] = 1.0
    if L1 > 0:
        Qn[:L1] -= h[i + 1:n]
        Qn[q] += h[j]
    if L2 > 0:
        Qn[:L2] += h[j + 1:n]
    if _LARGE_CORR:
        Qpos = Qn
        Ppos = Pn
        Qneg = Qs
        Pneg = Ps
    else:
        Qpos = Qs
        Ppos = Ps
        Qneg = Qn
        Pneg = Pn
    Rbuf[0] = T[c] - (-2.0 * x * x + 2.0 * s * x)
    Qbuf[0] = 2.0 * s
    Pbuf[0] = -2.0
    k = 1
    Tpos = T[n:2 * n - 1]
    Tneg = T[:n - 1][::-1]
    mp = (Ppos != 0.0) | (np.abs(Qpos) > 1e-15)
    cp = int(mp.sum())
    if cp:
        Pbuf[k:k + cp] = Ppos[mp]
        Qbuf[k:k + cp] = Qpos[mp]
        Rbuf[k:k + cp] = Tpos[mp] - (Ppos[mp] * x * x + Qpos[mp] * x)
        k += cp
    mn = (Pneg != 0.0) | (np.abs(Qneg) > 1e-15)
    cn = int(mn.sum())
    if cn:
        Pbuf[k:k + cn] = Pneg[mn]
        Qbuf[k:k + cn] = Qneg[mn]
        Rbuf[k:k + cn] = Tneg[mn] - (Pneg[mn] * x * x + Qneg[mn] * x)
        k += cn
    inc = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=bool)
    inc[c] = True
    if cp:
        inc[n + np.flatnonzero(mp)] = True
    if cn:
        inc[n - 1 - np.flatnonzero(mn)] = True
    Cuf = 0.0 if inc.all() else float(T[~inc].max())
    Pbuf[k] = 0.0
    Qbuf[k] = 0.0
    Rbuf[k] = Cuf
    k += 1
    low = Cuf - 1e-9
    if low < -1e-8:
        low = -1e-8
    high = C
    if high - low <= 1e-9:
        return False, C
    P = Pbuf[:k]
    Q = Qbuf[:k]
    R = Rbuf[:k]
    conv = P > 1e-12
    conc = P < -1e-12
    lin = ~(conv | conc)
    lpos = lin & (Q > 1e-14)
    lneg = lin & (Q < -1e-14)
    lz = lin & ~(lpos | lneg)
    qp = Q[lpos]
    rp = R[lpos]
    qn = Q[lneg]
    rn = R[lneg]
    rz = R[lz]
    cxp = P[conv]
    cxq = Q[conv]
    cxr = R[conv]
    cnp = P[conc]
    cnq = Q[conc]
    cnr = R[conc]
    for _ in range(32):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        if pair_check(mid, al, ah, qp, rp, qn, rn, rz, cxp, cxq, cxr, cnp, cnq, cnr):
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
        if high - low < 1e-12:
            break
    a = pair_choose(min(C, high + 1e-11), al, ah, x, qp, rp, qn, rn, rz, cxp, cxq, cxr, cnp, cnq, cnr)
    if a is None:
        return False, C
    a = min(max(a, al), ah)
    b = s - a
    if b < 0.0:
        b = 0.0
        a = s
    elif b > 1.0:
        b = 1.0
        a = s - 1.0
    if abs(a - x) < 1e-12:
        return False, C
    yold = y
    update_T_one(i, a, h, T, n)
    update_T_one(j, b, h, T, n)
    Cn = float(T.max())
    if Cn < C - 1e-12:
        return True, Cn
    update_T_one(j, yold, h, T, n)
    update_T_one(i, x, h, T, n)
    return False, C

def pair_sweep(h, T, n, C, pairs, limit, Qs, Qn, Ps, Pn, Pbuf, Qbuf, Rbuf):
    imp = 0
    for i, j in pairs:
        if time.time() > limit:
            break
        ok, Cn = attempt_pair(i, j, h, T, n, C, Qs, Qn, Ps, Pn, Pbuf, Qbuf, Rbuf)
        if ok:
            C = Cn
            imp += 1
    return C, imp

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 3
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
    except Exception:
        n = 256
        h = np.full(n, 0.5)
    if n < 8:
        n = 256
        h = np.full(n, 0.5)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    T = make_T(h, n)
    C = float(T.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = C
    best_n = n
    cap = 2 * n + 10
    Pbuf = np.empty(cap)
    Qbuf = np.empty(cap)
    Rbuf = np.empty(cap)
    Qs = np.empty(max(1, n - 1))
    Qn = np.empty(max(1, n - 1))
    Ps = np.empty(max(1, n - 1))
    Pn = np.empty(max(1, n - 1))

    def local_buffers():
        return Qs, Qn, Ps, Pn, Pbuf, Qbuf, Rbuf

    bufs = local_buffers()
    t1 = t0 + min(220, max(20.0, 0.22 * budget_s))
    stall = 0
    while time.time() < t1:
        order = rng.permutation(n - 1)
        pairs = list(zip(order.tolist(), [int(x) + 1 for x in order]))
        C, imp = pair_sweep(h, T, n, C, pairs, t1, *bufs)
        T = make_T(h, n)
        C = float(T.max())
        if C < best_c:
            best_c = C
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = n
        stall = 0 if imp else stall + 1
        if stall >= 5:
            break
    t2 = min(deadline - 150, t0 + max(350.0, 0.68 * budget_s))
    stall = 0
    while time.time() < t2:
        perm = rng.permutation(n).tolist()
        pairs = []
        for xx in range(n - 1):
            a = perm[xx]
            b = perm[xx + 1]
            if a != b:
                pairs.append((a, b))
        C, imp = pair_sweep(h, T, n, C, pairs, t2, *bufs)
        T = make_T(h, n)
        C = float(T.max())
        if C < best_c:
            best_c = C
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = n
        stall = 0 if imp else stall + 1
        if stall >= 8:
            break
    rest_end = deadline - max(25.0, 0.04 * budget_s)
    levels = [0.001, 0.002, 0.004, 0.008, 0.016, 0.03]
    for lvl in levels:
        avail = rest_end - time.time()
        if avail < 35.0:
            break
        phase_end = min(rest_end, time.time() + max(35.0, min(90.0, avail)))
        hp = best_h + lvl * rng.randn(n)
        hp = project_feasible(hp, n)
        Tp = make_T(hp, n)
        Cp = float(Tp.max())
        for _ in range(12):
            if time.time() > phase_end:
                break
            order = rng.permutation(n - 1)
            pairs = list(zip(order.tolist(), [int(x) + 1 for x in order]))
            Cp, imp = pair_sweep(hp, Tp, n, Cp, pairs, phase_end, *bufs)
            Tp = make_T(hp, n)
            Cp = float(Tp.max())
            if Cp < best_c:
                best_c = Cp
                best_h = hp.copy()
                best_n = n
                h = hp.copy()
                T = Tp.copy()
                C = Cp
            if imp == 0:
                break
    final_end = deadline - 3
    for _ in range(3):
        if time.time() > final_end:
            break
        order = rng.permutation(n - 1)
        pairs = list(zip(order.tolist(), [int(x) + 1 for x in order]))
        C, _ = pair_sweep(h, T, n, C, pairs, final_end, *bufs)
        T = make_T(h, n)
        C = float(T.max())
        if C < best_c:
            best_c = C
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = n
    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(best_h.sum() - 0.5 * best_n) > 1e-9:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    c5 = float(make_T(best_h, best_n).max())
    return best_h, c5, best_n
```
